What I did, step by step

  1. Swing and trend rules ran by code on 98 stocks (2026-11-03 08:24).
  2. Each stock went through its own category's rules (momentum, falling, range, IPO, steady).
  3. Fundamentals (2026-11-03): is it a real company, earnings date, PEG flags, floor.
  4. Monthly (2026-11-03): FINRA short interest 20261015, squeeze flags, seasonality, metals and rates.
  5. Daily (2026-11-03): gap, volume, averages, RSI, 7%+ two-day drops.
  6. Long-term names are stopped by not being a real company, earnings within 14 days, or 10%+ short interest. Swing names get those as warnings.
  7. My Trading Strategy, selling part (2026-11-03): trend, sell ladder and give-up level on every holding. Average-down rules ran on every holding.
